(wraps, not an error).\n\n**Grammar** (whitespace is *only* space and tab — `\\r`, `\\v`, `\\f` are errors):\n```\nexpr    := term  (('+'|'-') term)*            left assoc\nterm    := power (('*'|'/'|'%') power)*       left assoc\npower   := unary ('^' unary)*                 left assoc  ← not right\nunary   := ('+'|'-')* primary                 binds tighter than '^'\nprimary := NUMBER | '(' expr ')'\n```\nThe two non-conventional bits: `2^3^2` = **64** (`(2^3)^2`), and `-2^2` = **4** (`(-2)^2`), while `2^-3^2` = `(2^-3)^2` = 0.\n\n**Literals**: decimal digit runs (leading zeros are decimal, *not* octal — `0[REDACTED]0` = [REDACTED]0), or `0x`/`0X` + one or more hex digits (`0xFFFFFFFF` = -[REDACTED]). No `0b`, no `_`, no floats/exponents.\n\n**Division/remainder**: truncate toward zero, C-style — `-[REDACTED]0/3` = -3, `-[REDACTED]0%3` = -[REDACTED], `[REDACTED]0%-3` = [REDACTED]. Zero divisor → `ERR`.\n\n**Exponentiation**: repeated multiply with wraparound (`2^32` = 0). Negative exponent: `0` for any base except `[REDACTED]` → [REDACTED], `-[REDACTED]` → ±[REDACTED] by exponent parity, and base `0` → `ERR` (`0^-[REDACTED]` is an error but `2^-32` is just 0). `0^0` = [REDACTED].\n\n**Errors** → `ERR`: any syntax error, empty/blank input, unbalanced parens, `/` or `%` by zero, `0^negative`. Errors are eager — `0*([REDACTED]/0)` is `ERR`, no short-circuiting. Overflow is never an error.\n\nDeep input is handled by an iterative shunting-yard parser rather than recursion (the reference happily parses 2000-deep nesting; a recursive Python parser would blow the stack).","ttft_ms":[REDACTED]604,"type":"result","duration_ms":369[REDACTED]46,"uuid":"cb579eae-[REDACTED]a60-45f7-80[REDACTED]4-9705e0[REDACTED][REDACTED][REDACTED]e2b","ttft_stream_ms":[REDACTED][REDACTED]70,"time_to_request_ms":4[REDACTED],"first_content_frame_ms":[REDACTED][REDACTED]70,"queued_turn_count":0}
